- **panel**: Run competing positions against each other and come out with a DECISION, not a pile of opinions. - **paper-deck**: 综述 PPT 的生成规范：单篇论文 A/B 两页结构（页A 论据 + 页B 配图与解读）、逐字搬运不创作、 pptxgenjs 排版规范与已知坑、QA 检查表。适用于解读确认后进入写作阶段、或用户要求调整 PPT 版式时。触发词：生成 PPT、做幻灯片、A/B 结构、排版、deck。 ([zeros335882878/dsh-paper-survey](https://github.com/zeros335882878/dsh-paper-survey/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-deck))
- **paper-deck-style**: Minimal, spacious, high-end slide design for Paper boards. Use when creating or extending presentations in Paper that should feel keynote-like: slightly off-white backgrounds, Apple-like typography, one strong idea per slide, real UI/diagram graphics instead of text-heavy explanation, soft muted accents, obvious visual flows, and subtle section style shifts. ([ruicore/codex-skills](https://github.com/ruicore/codex-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-deck-style) · ★2)
- **paper-decomposition**: Decomposes research papers into structured knowledge graphs including research questions, methodology chains, claims hierarchy, and evidence graphs. ([LittleSongxx/SoulSearcher](https://github.com/LittleSongxx/SoulSearcher/tree/HEAD/skills/public/paper-decomposition) · ★3)
- **paper-deconstruct**: 论文论证结构恢复器——把一篇论文重建为可追溯的研究结构（quote 全程机器校验），产出两页 paper-map、evidence-ledger 和 5–10 个真正需要人判断的问题。不是论文总结器。输入可以是单个 PDF/md/txt，也可以是一个装着多篇 PDF 的目录（每篇独立处理）；交付物落在论文自己所在目录下的同名子目录里，不建独立的输出树。Use when the user asks to 拆论文 / deconstruct a paper (or a folder of papers) / run paper-deconstruct on a PDF, markdown file, or directory. Modes — triage (10 分钟判断值不值得深拆) / full (完整重建). ([HappyEpicDragon/paper-deconstruct](https://github.com/HappyEpicDragon/paper-deconstruct/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **paper-deep-analysis**: Deeply analyzes a research paper from a local PDF, public PDF URL, paper link, arXiv URL, or arXiv ID. Use when the user asks to read, critique, explain, or learn from one paper and wants an evidence-grounded human-readable HTML report plus structured Agent-learning JSONL. Selects a paper-specific research perspective such as Agent evaluation, Harness, Memory, continual learning, or Data Agent. Refuses paywall bypass, private-network fetching, and unsupported image-only PDFs. ([Icey-Lan/paper-deep-analysis-skill](https://github.com/Icey-Lan/paper-deep-analysis-skill/tree/HEAD/dsh/assets) · ★1)
- **paper-deep-analysis**: Deeply analyzes a research paper from a local PDF, public PDF URL, paper link, arXiv URL, or arXiv ID. Use when the user asks to read, critique, explain, compare, or learn from one paper and wants an evidence-grounded human-readable HTML report plus structured Agent-learning JSONL. Selects a paper-specific research perspective such as Agent evaluation, Harness, Memory, continual learning, or Data Agent. Refuses paywall bypass, private-network fetching, and unsupported image-only PDFs. ([Icey-Lan/paper-deep-analysis-skill](https://github.com/Icey-Lan/paper-deep-analysis-skill/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/paper-deep-analysis) · ★1)
- **paper-deep-dive**: 论文深度解读——Fact/Inference 分离、方法论、SOTA 对比、Reviewer#2 批判、工业落地评估、面试压缩版。 ([jinglong92/longClaw](https://github.com/jinglong92/longClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/learn-paper-deep-dive) · ★4)
- **paper-deep-dive**: Canonical single-paper deep-dive delivery standard, with Notion as the durable target. Use whenever the user mentions deep dive, 深读, 详细解析, detailed-read, dive into a paper, full paper reading, 原文中译稿, or asks to audit/repair a deep-dive package. Produces one self-contained Notion main page whose body is only the complete 原文中译稿. Do not add a 精读稿, 精读部分, Paper Card, 论文解析树, close reading, or mechanism synthesis unless the user explicitly requests that artifact in the current task. English-manuscript and close-reading pages are optional only when explicitly requested. - **paper-deep-presentation**: 精读一篇论文时补足必要背景并生成独立术语表，重建问题、最小子问题、对应子方法、基础理论、从 insight 到实验场景的设计链和有限结论，并抽象可迁移的问题—方法原则；需要时提出可反驳的新想法。可用于 PDF、论文链接、现有 Obsidian 笔记或学术报告准备。 ([ljhOfGithub/mydotcodex](https://github.com/ljhOfGithub/mydotcodex/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-deep-presentation))
- **paper-deep-read**: Guide evidence-grounded deep readings of one or more research papers, including a scoped field landscape, paper identity and version checks, method and equation walkthroughs, official-code implementation analysis, key original figures, critical evaluation, interactive comprehension checks, and a polished verified Notion reading page.
